# Household hh_s48 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, dining, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Marco** (resident_1): a student with classes out; untidy (tidiness 0.40); punctual (jitter scale 0.83); often forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.39); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the office desk. Hobbies: baking, family_calls, sketching. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Priya** (resident_2): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.68); average timing (jitter scale 1.08); rarely forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.60); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. ## Day 1 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d1`: Marco is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:20–16:50).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d1`: Marco gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:02–19:47).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d1`: Marco has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
